Dear Siva Ram Sir,

fi

‘With reference to our discussion, please find attached the following reference documents to support the Al-based Merchandising Work Automation initiative.

Buyer Email Copy ~ Sample customer order details received through email.

CAD Sheet — Reference for size-wise order details and related requirements.

Merchandising Manual Excel File - Current manual working process followed by the Merchandising Department.

Axpert Order Entry Screen ~ Details of the fields and parameters currently captured during order creation

Axpert Summary Details - Existing ERP summary output for reference and validation

‘These documents are shared to help your team understand the current order intake and order creation process, identify manual activities, and evaluate the opportunities for Al-based automation.

Kindly review the attached documents and map the existing manual workflow against the Axpert process. The objective is to automate data extraction, validation, and preparation of order details, reduce

repetitive manual work, minimize data-entry errors, and ensure that the final output matches the existing ERP requirements.

As discussed, we can take one customer order as a pilot for Phase 1 and validate the complete process before proceeding with further automation.

Please let me know if any additional information or clarification is required from my side.

Thanks / Regards,

S. Ramesh

‘With reference to our discussion, please find attached the following reference documents to support the Al-based Merchandising Work Automation initiative.

Buyer Email Copy ~ Sample customer order details received through email.

CAD Sheet ~ Reference for size-wise order details and related requirements.

Merchandising Manual Excel File ~ Current manual working process followed by the Merchandising Department.

Axpert Order Entry Screen ~ Details of the fields and parameters currently captured during order creation

Axpert Summary Details — Existing ERP summary output for reference and validation

These documents are shared to help your team understand the current order intake and oyder creation process, identify manual activities, and evaluate the opportunities for Al-based automation.

Kindly review the attached documents and map the existing manual workflow against the Axpert process. The objective is to automate data extraction, validation, and preparation of order details, reduce

repetitive manual work, minimize data-entry errors, and ensure that the final output matches the existing ERP requirements.

As discussed, we can take one customer order as a pilot for Phase 1 and validate the complete process before proceeding with further automation.

Please let me know if any additional information or clarification is required from my side.

Thanks / Regards,

Dear Siva Ram Sir,

‘With reference to our discussion, please find attached the following reference documents to support the Al-based Merchandising Work Automation initiative.

Buyer Email Copy — Sample customer order details received through email.

CAD Sheet - Reference for size-wise order details and related requirements.

Merchandising Manual Excel File ~ Current manual working process followed by the Merchandising Department.

Axpert Order Entry Screen ~ Details of the fields and parameters currently captured during order creation

Axpert Summary Details - Existing ERP summary output for reference and validation

These documents are shared to help your team understand the current order intake and order Yeation process, identify manual activities, and evaluate the opportunities for Al-based automation.

Kindly review the attached documents and map the existing manual workflow against the Axpert process. The objective is to automate data extraction, validation, and preparation of order details, reduce

repetitive manual work, minimize data-entry errors, and ensure that the final output matches the existing ERP requirements.

As discussed, we can take one customer order as a pilot for Phase 1 and validate the complete process before proceeding with further automation.

Please let me know if any additional information or clarification is required from my side.

Thanks / Regards,

S. Ramesh
